# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-10-19)

## Corpus Check
- 4141 files · ~10,272,799 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 95 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 30, .css 15, .entitlements 8)

## Summary
- 60847 nodes · 174821 edges · 1247 communities (1138 shown, 109 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 7095 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `9ae5de0a`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
